Abstract: Ensemble Kalman Inversion (EKI) is a derivative-free, ensemble-based method for inverse and optimization problems. Its continuous-time formulation can be interpreted as an interacting particle system driven by a Kalman-type preconditioned descent direction. A well-known limitation of this dynamics is the possible premature collapse of the covariance of the ensemble, which makes the method sensitive to the initial ensemble.
  We introduce a second-order particle system in which the particles evolve according to an inertial dynamics. The model combines a Kalman-type relaxation force with damping, attraction towards the ensemble mean, and a short-range repulsive interaction designed to counteract ensemble collapse. The resulting dynamics can be interpreted as a heavy-ball reformulation of continuous-time EKI enriched by competing attractive and repulsive mechanisms.
  For linear inverse problems, we analyze the induced mean and fluctuation dynamics and identify a parameter regime in which fully collapsed configurations are linearly unstable. We further characterize asymptotic equilibria through a constrained optimality condition on the subspace retained by the limiting ensemble covariance and derive an exponential decay estimate. Numerical experiments illustrate the effect of inertia and repulsion on the ensemble dynamics and compare the proposed second-order method with first-order EKI-type

</details>


### [137] [A Microlocal Open-Boundary Method for Residual-Based Wave Solvers on Unbounded Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06115)
*Avy Soffer,Nguyen Gia Hien,Minh-Binh Tran*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种微局域相空间滤波物理信息神经网络（PINN-TDPSF）框架，用于无界域上的波传播问题。该方法将内部演化的逐层神经残差逼近与计算域周围缓冲区中的时变相空间滤波器相结合，通过相空间辐射机制替代传统人工边界条件，仅在波包靠近边界且群速度向外时才将其移除。数值实验表明该方法能有效减少人工反射和环绕效应，适用于线性/非线性薛定谔方程、各向异性介质、线性化欧拉波及稀疏数据缺陷恢复等多种场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无界域波传播问题中，传统人工边界条件（如吸收边界层、完美匹配层）在处理复杂介质、非线性、未知系数或非FFT对角化物理时存在局限性。现有方法如FFT、谱方法或分步求解器虽在已知系数正问题中高度精确，但无法灵活结合非线性残差、稀疏/非网格观测、未知系数或可变内部介质。因此需要一种新的神经框架，将时变相空间滤波嵌入残差网络，以克服上述限制。

Method: 采用逐层神经残差逼近（slabwise neural residual approximation）模拟内部演化，同时在计算域周围的缓冲区（buffer zone）施加时变相空间滤波器（time-dependent phase-space filter）。该滤波器的核心思想是相空间辐射机制：仅当波包局域化于人工边界附近且其群速度指向外部时才移除该分量，而非采用传统的局部人工边界惩罚项。该方法将相空间滤波嵌入残差神经网络框架中。

Result: 数值实验涵盖线性薛定谔传播、势散射、各向异性薛定谔动力学、非线性薛定谔波包、孤子应力测试、线性化欧拉波以及基于稀疏数据的局域声学缺陷恢复。结果表明该方法：1）有效减少人工反射和环绕效应；2）在各向异性介质中正确使用群速度；3）保留物理上入射分支分量；4）当出射波包滤波假设被违反时提供诊断信息。

Conclusion: PINN-TDPSF框架为无界域波传播提供了一种灵活的神经求解方案，特别适用于传统FFT、谱方法或分步求解器无法直接处理的问题（如非线性残差、未知系数、稀疏/非网格观测、可变内部介质等）。该方法的相空间辐射机制替代了传统人工边界条件，在多种波动问题中均表现出良好的吸收性能和物理一致性。

Abstract: We introduce a microlocal phase-space-filtered physics-informed neural network (PINN--TDPSF or Microlocal PINNFilter) framework for wave propagation on unbounded domains. The method combines a slabwise neural residual approximation of the interior evolution with a time-dependent phase-space filter applied in a buffer surrounding the physical computational domain. The central idea is to replace local artificial-boundary penalties by a phase-space radiation mechanism: a component is removed only when it is localized near the artificial boundary and its group velocity points outward.
  The proposed method is not intended to replace FFT, spectral, or split-step solvers for known-coefficient forward problems where such methods are available and highly accurate. Instead, it embeds the time-dependent phase-space filter into a residual-based neural framework. This coupling is useful when open-domain wave propagation must be combined with nonlinear residuals, sparse or off-grid observations, unknown coefficients, variable interior media, or other non-FFT-diagonalizable physics. Numerical experiments for linear Schrödinger propagation, potential scattering, anisotropic Schrödinger dynamics, nonlinear Schrödinger wave packets, soliton stress tests, linearized Euler waves, and sparse-data recovery of a localized acoustic defect show that the method reduces artificial reflection and wraparound, uses group velocity correctly in anisotropic media, preserves physically incoming branch components, and provides diagnostics when the assumptions behind outgoing-packet filtering are violated.

</details>


### [138] [A q-Tsallis Safe Approximation for Chance-Constrained Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06401)
*Sergio Assunção Monteiro,Fabricio Alves Barbosa da Silva*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Tsallis统计流形几何的q-CCP方法（非广延机会约束规划），从黎曼几何视角证明q-CVaR是尾部单纯形面的$g^{(q)}$-测地线投影，给出q-CCP比传统CVaR-CCP严格收紧的理论保证，并通过迭代LP实现（2-3轮收敛）。实证方面，在Ibovespa股票和M5库存报童问题中分别达到$q^*=1.50,1.88$，实现零缺货违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统经验CVaR机会约束规划在重尾分布下系统性地低估尾部事件，现有方法缺乏几何可解释性且难以自动调整安全裕度。

Method: 基于Tsallis统计流形的黎曼几何框架，引入非广延q-CVaR泛函（秩排序q-CVaR escort权重为$g^{(q)}$-测地线投影），构建q-CCP可行域为Tsallis散度球，通过迭代线性规划求解。

Result: 理论：q-CCP是CVaR-CCP的严格收紧（定理7）；经验违规比$\rho(q)=[1-(1-\varepsilon)^{q+1}]/\varepsilon$与尾指数$\nu$无关（命题10）；可行域体积成本随$q$和$\nu$单调递增（命题11）。实证：Ibovespa股票违规比0.241（$q^*=1.50$）；M5库存零缺货违规，成本溢价1.155倍。

Conclusion: q-CCP提供了数据自适应的安全调节旋钮，兼具凸性和一致性，能有效处理重尾分布下的机会约束问题，在金融和供应链场景中验证了有效性。

Abstract: Classical chance-constrained programs are solved by safe approximations based on the empirical CVaR, which uses a uniform measure over scenarios and systematically underweights tail events under heavy-tailed distributions. We introduce \emph{q-CCP}, a non-extensive safe approximation grounded in the Riemannian geometry of the Tsallis statistical manifold: the rank-based q-CVaR escort weights are the $g^{(q)}$-geodesic projection onto the tail simplex face, and the q-CCP feasible set is a Tsallis-divergence ball (Proposition~12). This geometric foundation yields three results. First, q-CCP is a provable strict tightening of CVaR-CCP for all $q > 1$ (Theorem~7). Second, the empirical violation ratio satisfies $ρ(q) = [1-(1-\varepsilon)^{q+1}]/\varepsilon$, independent of the tail index $ν$ (Proposition~10). Third, the feasible-region volume cost is monotone increasing in $q$ and $ν$ (Proposition~11), providing a data-adaptive safety knob. The formulation inherits convexity and coherence from the q-CVaR functional and admits an iterative LP reformulation converging in 2--3 iterations. Experiments on 15 Ibovespa equities confirm the theory (violation ratio $0.241$, $q^* = 1.50$); an M5 inventory newsvendor experiment generalises the method to supply chain ($q^* = 1.88$, cost premium $1.155\times$, zero OOS stockout violations).

</details>


### [139] [Post-processed frozen-flow methods for the long time sampling of ergodic dynamics on Riemannian manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06150)
*Adrien Busnot Laurent,Sébastien Macé*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于黎曼流形上遍历随机微分方程（包括黎曼朗之万动力学）的新型内蕴逼近方法，仅依赖测地线、平行移动等自然几何运算，无需嵌入或坐标。通过开发新型内蕴数值方法（结合奇异李-布彻级数），在不变测度采样上实现了高阶精度，并在长时采样效率上超越传统外蕴与内蕴方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有外蕴方法（如惩罚法、投影法）依赖嵌入或坐标，在流形上效率低且不自然。需要一种仅依赖流形固有几何结构的内蕴方法，以提升长时采样中不变测度的逼近精度与计算效率。

Method: 利用测地线和并行传输等自然几何操作构造内蕴数值格式；通过奇异李-布彻级数推导高阶条件；借鉴Leimkuhler-Matthews方法思想，优先保证长时采样效率而非有限时间精度。

Result: 给出了不变测度高阶精度的判据；新方法在同等精度下计算成本显著低于现有外蕴和内蕴方法；数值实验验证了优越性。

Conclusion: 纯几何内蕴方法在黎曼流形上的SDE采样中兼具高阶精度与高效性，为复杂流形上的朗之万动力学模拟提供了新范式。

Abstract: In this work, we propose a novel intrinsic approach to the approximation of ergodic SDEs on Riemannian manifolds, which include Riemannian Langevin dynamics. In opposition to the standard extrinsic approaches such as penalization methods and projection methods, our methodology does not use embeddings or coordinates and only relies on natural geometric operations: geodesics, parallel transport,... We give a criterion for high order of accuracy for the invariant measure, develop new intrinsic numerical methods designed solely for sampling the invariant measure, and derive high order conditions using a new algebraic operation on exotic Lie-Butcher series. In the spirit of the Leimkuhler-Matthews method, our approach prioritizes long time sampling efficiency over finite time accuracy, and outperforms the previous extrinsic and intrinsic approaches in terms of cost for a given accuracy, which we illustrate with several numerical experiments.

</details>


### [140] [Structure-Preserving Operator Splitting via JR-Decomposition for Circuit Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06153)
*Andreas Bartel,Malak Diab*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文在端口-哈密顿框架下研究改进节点分析（MNA）电路模型，针对标准JR分解在电路应用中受限的问题，提出了增强型JR分解方法，并通过数值算例验证了该方法的适用性、收敛性和结构保持特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准JR分解在电路模型（MNA）中的应用受到限制，无法有效实现能量共形分裂，因此需要一种针对电路特性专门设计的改进分解方法。

Method: 在端口-哈密顿（port-Hamiltonian）框架下研究MNA电路模型，提出增强型JR分解（enhanced JR-decomposition），放松标准JR分解的限制条件以适配电路应用场景。

Result: 通过数值示例验证了所提增强型JR分解方法的适用性、收敛性以及结构保持性质。

Conclusion: 增强型JR分解能够有效应用于MNA电路模型，在保持能量共形分裂的基础上解决了标准分解的局限性，具有结构保持和收敛性保障的优势。

Abstract: We investigate circuit models, namely, modified nodal analysis (MNA) in the port-Hamiltonian framework. Based on this, the JR-decomposition for the numerical treatment would offer an energy conform splitting. However, for circuit models, the application of the standard JR-decomposition is restricted. To enable a JR-decomposition for MNA, we need to relax the decomposition. To this end, we introduce the enhanced JR-decomposition, which is particularly tailored to the application to circuits. We conclude with a numerical example that illustrates the applicability of the proposed approach as well as its convergence and structure-preserving properties.

</details>


### [141] [Quantum Algorithm for Nonlinear and Stochastic Homogenization via a Young-Measure based Linear Programming Formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06165)
*Siqi Chen,Shi Jin,Lei Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文通过基于Young测度的线性规划（LP）公式研究非线性和随机均匀化的量子算法，将非线性问题提升到更高维度的线性问题，在确定性设置中实现多项式量子加速，在随机设置中实现随机采样成本的平方根缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，非线性和随机均匀化问题需要直接解析细尺度振荡，计算代价高昂。本文旨在利用Young测度将非线性问题提升为高维线性问题，从而借助量子LP求解器实现加速，避免直接处理细尺度复杂性。

Method: 采用Young测度将非线性/随机均匀化问题转化为高维线性规划问题，将微尺度、梯度和随机变量作为独立变量处理。在确定性场景中利用量子LP求解器获得多项式加速；在随机场景中通过将所有随机实现编码到单一LP中，获得量子平方根加速。

Result: 确定性均匀化在中等精度需求下实现多项式量子加速；随机均匀化实现随随机变量数量增长的平方根采样成本缩减；Young测度的正则性或稀疏性可进一步扩展优势至精细精度；一维和二维数值实验验证了LP公式的正确性。

Conclusion: 基于Young测度的LP公式为非线性和随机均匀化问题提供了可量子加速的计算框架，在中等精度和随机维度较高时优势明显，为量子计算在科学计算中的应用开辟了新途径。

Abstract: We study quantum algorithms for nonlinear and stochastic homogenization via a Young-measure based linear programming (LP) formulation, which lifts the nonlinear problem to a linear one in higher dimensions by treating the microscale, the gradient, and possible random variables as independent variables, thereby capturing effective macroscopic quantities without directly resolving fine-scale oscillations. The resulting LP is large but structured, and its high-dimensional nature creates regimes in which quantum LP solvers outperform direct classical solvers: in the deterministic setting, polynomial quantum speedup arises when moderate homogenized accuracy suffices; in the stochastic setting, encoding all random realizations simultaneously in a single LP yields a quantum square-root reduction in stochastic sampling cost that grows with the number of random variables. Regularity or sparsity of the Young measure may further extend these advantages to fine-scale accuracy. Numerical experiments on one- and two-dimensional benchmarks confirm the correctness of the Young-measure LP formulation.

</details>


### [142] [Error Analysis of Tr-PINNs Algorithm for 2D Incompressible Navier-Stokes Equations with Non-Homogeneous Boundary Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06268)
*Dongjie Liu,Xuebo Li,Rong Yang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 针对传统PINNs在处理非齐次边界条件的Navier-Stokes方程时仅用L²范数约束边界项导致模拟不准确的问题，本文提出Tr-PINNs方法，通过校正边界值误差提升预测精度，并基于非齐次Stokes问题建立了算法误差分析，数值实验验证了该方法在计算精度上的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PINNs在处理带非齐次边界条件的Navier-Stokes方程时，仅使用L²范数约束边界项，这种单一的约束策略可能导致边界模拟不准确，进而降低整体预测精度。

Method: 提出Tr-PINNs（修正边界值误差的改进物理信息神经网络），通过校正边界值误差来改进传统PINNs，并基于非齐次Stokes问题建立了算法的误差分析理论。

Result: 数值实验表明，Tr-PINNs算法在计算精度上取得了显著提升。

Conclusion: Tr-PINNs通过修正边界值误差有效解决了传统PINNs在非齐次边界条件下边界模拟不准确的问题，显著提高了预测精度。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) have been widely applied to solve Navier-Stokes equations by enforcing outputs and gradients of deep models to satisfy target equations. However, conventional PINNs only constrain the boundary terms by means of the $L^2$-norm when addressing the equations with non-homogeneous boundary conditions. This single constraint strategy may cause inaccurate boundary simulation, further resulting in the decline of prediction accuracy. To resolve this critical issue, this paper proposes an improved physics-informed neural network by correcting the error of the boundary value, which is called Tr-PINNs. Based on the results of nonhomogeneous Stokes problem, the algorithm error analysis of Tr-PINNs is established. The efficacy of the Tr-PINNs algorithm is demonstrated via numerical experiments, which further demonstrate that the Tr-PINNs algorithm achieves a remarkable improvement in computational accuracy.

</details>


### [143] [DAS-PINNs for high-dimensional partial differential equations: extending deep adaptive sampling to spacetime domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06314)
*Anshima Singh,David J. Silvester*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 针对高维时空域中具有局部和动态演化解的PDE问题，本文将深度自适应采样框架扩展到时间依赖场景，使用归一化流模型学习PDE残差分布以生成新的配点，在无需显式时间推进的情况下自动追踪高残差区域，并在2至8维问题上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维时空域中具有空间局部性和动态演化解的时间依赖PDE对PINNs构成根本性挑战，因为均匀配点采样在高维时空域中效率极低，亟需有效的自适应采样方法。

Method: 将时空视为统一域（无显式时间推进），利用归一化流神经网络模型学习PDE残差诱导的分布，生成集中于难以学习区域的新配点，由PDE残差分布驱动自动识别和追踪时空中的高残差区域。

Result: 在2维到8维空间的一系列基准问题上验证了方法有效性，包括尖锐运动特征和局部化结构等场景。

Conclusion: 所提出的深度自适应采样框架能够无需显式时间步进或移动网格，自动识别和追踪时空中的高残差区域，有效提升PINNs在高维时变PDE问题上的求解能力。

Abstract: Time-dependent high-dimensional partial differential equations (PDEs) with spatially localised and dynamically evolving solutions pose a fundamental challenge for physics-informed neural networks (PINNs), as uniform collocation sampling becomes increasingly ineffective in high-dimensional spatiotemporal domains. In this work, a deep adaptive sampling framework for PINNs is extended to the time-dependent setting by treating space and time as a unified domain without any explicit time marching. A normalising flow neural network model effectively learns the distribution induced by the PDE residual and generates new collocation points concentrated in regions where the solution is most difficult to learn. Unlike conventional adaptive strategies that require explicit time stepping or moving meshes, high-residual regions are automatically identified and tracked across both space and time, driven purely by the PDE residual distribution. The effectiveness of the proposed strategy is assessed on a range of benchmark problems, from sharp and moving features in two spatial dimensions to localised structures in up to eight spatial dimensions.

</details>

### [144] [RedZeD: Computing persistent homology by Reduction to Zero Differentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06310)
*Chris Kapulkin,Nathan Kershaw*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的 Vietoris–Rips 滤形持续同调计算算法，通过"主动枚举"和"归约到零微分（RedZeD）"理论框架，在多数情况下显著提升了现有持续配对算法的速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续同调计算算法在 Vietoris–Rips 滤形上效率不足，需要更高效的方法来处理大规模数据。

Method: 提出了主动枚举（active enumeration）技术，并建立了归约到零微分（Reduction to Zero Differentials, RedZeD）理论框架，在此基础上设计了新的持续同调计算算法。

Result: 新算法在 Vietoris–Rips 滤形的持续同调计算中，相比现有实现获得了显著的加速效果。

Conclusion: RedZeD 框架和主动枚举技术为持续同调的高效计算提供了新的理论视角和实践方法，有望推动拓扑数据分析在大规模数据集上的应用。

Abstract: We introduce a new algorithm for computing persistent homology of Vietoris--Rips filtrations, which in many cases offers a considerable speedup over the existing implementation of the persistence pairing algorithm. The key innovation, called active enumeration, is made possible by a new theoretical framework of Reduction to Zero Differentials (hence RedZeD) in which to view persistent homology.

</details>


### [145] [Analytic patch trees: branch interface inheritance and fractal dimension fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06400)
*Henk Mulder*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文将解析分形曲线树扩展到解析曲面片树，发现分支点被接口曲线取代，接口曲线将父片元的完整解析状态传递给子片元。建立了曲面片树的可积性和适定性条件，并引入了共形性约束。证明了曲面片树具有自然的叶状结构，可切分为一维曲线树，每个曲线树有其豪斯多夫维数，共同构成光滑的维数场。将二维曲面模型推广到任意维度n，其中n-1个接口流形传递父片元的n维场状态。片元场维数与分支演化维数之间的平衡或差异决定了从几何性到操作性的分析机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将解析分形曲线树推广到解析曲面片树，探索更高维度的几何结构，特别是接口曲线在决定曲面片树的拓扑、自相似性条件以及维数场中的核心作用。

Method: 建立曲面片树的可积性和适定性解析条件；引入共形性约束；通过自然叶状结构将曲面片树分解为一维曲线树；将模型推广到任意维度n，定义n-1维接口流形传输父片元的n维场状态。

Result: 发现了接口曲线替代分支点这一新几何结构；证明了曲面片树可分解为具有各自豪斯多夫维数的一维曲线树，形成光滑维数场；建立了片元场维数与分支演化维数之间的平衡关系决定分析机制的性质。

Conclusion: 曲面片树扩展了分形几何的研究范畴，接口流形是决定高维分形树拓扑和自相似性的核心结构，维数平衡关系实现了从几何到操作的分析机制分类。

Abstract: The extension of the analytic fractal curve trees of (2601.17490} to analytic surface patch trees reveals a new geometric structure: branch points are replaced by interface curves that transmit the full analytical state of parent patches to their children. These interfaces prove to be central in determining the topology of the surface patch trees, including for the conditions for self-similarity of the interfaces, the patches and thus the trees.
  We establish the analytic conditions for the integrability and well-posedness of the surface patch trees and introduce further restrictions for conformality. We demonstrate that patch trees have a natural foliation that slices the trees into one dimensional curve trees, each of which has their own Hausdorff dimension, jointly creating a smooth dimension field.
  We extend the two dimensional surface model to arbitrary dimensions $n$ where $n-1$ interface manifolds transport the $n$ field state of the parent patches to their child branches. We note that the balance or discrepancy between patch field dimension and the dimensions in which the branches may evolve, determine the analytical regime from essentially geometrical to essentially operational.

</details>

# stat.AP [[Back]](#toc)

### [146] [Eigenvector Spatial Filters Nuclear Norm Matrix Completion with Application to Air Quality Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05450)
*Rodolfo Metulini*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出ESFNNMC方法，通过在核范数矩阵补全中引入Moran型特征向量来捕捉空间自相关，相比传统固定效应方法显著提升了环境面板数据中缺失观测值的插补精度，且计算成本基本不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的核范数矩阵补全方法在环境面板数据插补中忽略了空气质量过程固有的空间依赖性，导致插补精度受限。

Method: 提出ESFNNMC方法，在核范数固定效应矩阵补全基础上，用Moran型特征向量替代单元特定截距项以捕捉空间自相关；采用块坐标下降法（BCD）求解多凸优化问题，结合软阈值奇异值分解和交叉验证正则化。

Result: 在多种缺失模式、时空自相关水平、矩阵维度、形状和秩结构的模拟中，ESFNNMC相比标准固定效应方法显著提升了插补精度，且计算成本基本不变。应用于2021年意大利伦巴第大区64个监测站的日PM10数据插补。

Conclusion: ESFNNMC能够有效利用空间依赖结构提升环境面板数据缺失值插补的可靠性，为暴露评估和政策分析提供了更准确的工具。

Abstract: Reliable reconstruction of missing observations in environmental panel datasets is essential for accurate exposure assessment and policy analysis. Traditional nuclear norm matrix completion methods effectively impute missing entries in low-rank matrices, yet often overlook the spatial dependence inherent to air quality processes. This paper introduces the Eigenvector Spatial Filters Nuclear Norm Matrix Completion (ESFNNMC) method, an extension of nuclear norm fixed-effects matrix completion that replaces unit-specific intercepts with a set of Moran-type eigenvectors capturing spatial autocorrelation in the data. To estimate the model, we propose a Block-Coordinate Descent (BCD) approach for multiconvex optimization problems, with soft-thresholded singular value decomposition and cross-validated regularization. Through comprehensive simulations varying missingness patterns, the level of spatial and temporal autocorrelation, and dimension, shape, and rank structure of the matrices, ESFNNMC demonstrates substantial improvements in imputation accuracy over the standard fixed-effects approach, while keeping the computational cost approximately unchanged. The method is applied to impute missing entries in daily PM10 measurements in 64 monitoring stations in Lombardy, Italy, during the year 2021.

</details>
